                    let _ = Self::inject_device_immunity(serial, None);
                    return Ok(format!(
                        "📱 App Agente Esfera v3 instalado e ativado no celular {} com sucesso!",
                        serial
                    ));
                }
            }

            Err(format!(
                "Falha na instalação do APK no celular {}: {}",
                serial,
                combined.trim()
            ))
        }
    }

    /// Monitor de status: marca devices como offline se check-in expirou
    /// Reconecta automaticamente qualquer device offline que tenha CurrentAdbAddress
    pub fn start_status_monitor(db_conn: std::sync::Arc<crate::db::Database>) {
        use std::collections::HashSet;
        let injected: std::sync::Arc<std::sync::Mutex<HashSet<String>>> =
            std::sync::Arc::new(std::sync::Mutex::new(HashSet::new()));

        std::thread::spawn(move || loop {
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30));

            // 1. Marca offline devices com check-in expirado (>120s sem heartbeat)
            let _ = db_conn.mark_stale_devices_offline(120);

            // 2. Reconecta todo device offline que tenha CurrentAdbAddress (com ou sem EsferaDeviceId)
            let devices = db_conn.get_devices(1);
            for dev in devices {
                let has_checkin = dev.last_seen_utc.is_some();
                let is_online = dev.status == "online";

                if is_online {
                    // Preventivo: devices online também tentam reconnect no CurrentAdbAddress
                    if let Some(ref addr) = dev.current_adb_address {
                        if addr.contains(':') && addr.contains('.') {
                            let mut adb = create_cmd("adb");
                            adb.arg("connect").arg(addr);
                            let _ = run_cmd_timeout(adb, Duration::from_secs(8));
                        }
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                // Device offline — tenta reconnect pelo CurrentAdbAddress, IP ou serial
                let target = if let Some(ref addr) = dev.current_adb_address {
                    if addr.contains(':') && addr.contains('.') {
                        addr.clone()
                    } else if !dev.ip.is_empty() && dev.ip.contains('.') {
                        format!("{}:{}", dev.ip, dev.port)
                    } else if dev.serial.contains(':') {
                        dev.serial.clone()
                    } else {
                        continue;
                    }
                } else if !dev.ip.is_empty() && dev.ip.contains('.') {
                    format!("{}:{}", dev.ip, dev.port)
                } else if dev.serial.contains(':') {
                    dev.serial.clone()
                } else {
                    continue;
                };

                let mut adb = create_cmd("adb");
                adb.arg("connect").arg(&target);
                let _ = run_cmd_timeout(adb, Duration::from_secs(8));

                // Se conectou, marca online
                if let Ok(active) = Self::list_devices() {
                    let ip_part = target.split(':').next().unwrap_or(&target);
                    if active.iter().any(|d| d.serial == target || d.serial.contains(ip_part)) {
                        let _ = db_conn.mark_device_online(dev.id, &target);
                    }
                }

                let already = injected
                    .lock()
                    .map(|s| s.contains(&target))
                    .unwrap_or(false);
                if !already {
                    let pid = format!("ESFERA_DEV_{:03}", dev.device_number);
                    if Self::inject_device_immunity(&target, Some(&pid)).is_ok() {
                        let _ = injected.lock().map(|mut s| {
                            s.insert(target.clone());
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /// Obtém a resolução da tela física do dispositivo (Physical size: WxH)
    pub fn get_device_screen_size(serial: &str) -> (i32, i32) {
        let output = create_cmd("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("wm")
            .arg("size")
            .output();

        if let Ok(out) = output {
            let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
            for line in stdout.lines() {
                if line.contains("Physical size:") || line.contains("Override size:") {
                    if let Some(res) = line.split(':').nth(1) {
                        let parts: Vec<&str> = res.trim().split('x').collect();
                        if parts.len() == 2 {
                            if let (Ok(w), Ok(h)) = (parts[0].trim().parse::<i32>(), parts[1].trim().parse::<i32>()) {
                                return (w, h);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        (1080, 2400) // Fallback seguro padrão
    }

    /// Encoder de URL para parâmetros do Intent
    pub fn url_encode(input: &str) -> String {
        let mut encoded = String::new();
        for byte in input.bytes() {
            match byte {
                b'A'..=b'Z' | b'a'..=b'z' | b'0'..=b'9' | b'-' | b'_' | b'.' | b'~' => {
                    encoded.push(byte as char);
                }
                b' ' => encoded.push_str("%20"),
                _ => {
                    encoded.push_str(&format!("%{:02X}", byte));
                }
            }
        }
        encoded
    }

    /// Envia mensagem no WhatsApp via Deep Link Intent + Toque Físico Curva de Bézier C++ HPC
    pub fn send_whatsapp_intent_and_bezier_tap(serial: &str, phone: &str, message: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let encoded_msg = Self::url_encode(message);
        let intent_url = format!("https://api.whatsapp.com/send?phone={}&text={}", phone, encoded_msg);

        // 1. Dispara Intent do WhatsApp
        let mut intent_adb = create_cmd("adb");
        intent_adb
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("am")
            .arg("start")
            .arg("-a")
            .arg("android.intent.action.VIEW")
            .arg("-d")
            .arg(&intent_url);
        let intent_cmd = run_cmd_timeout(intent_adb, Duration::from_secs(8));

        if let Err(e) = intent_cmd {
            return Err(format!("Falha ao disparar Intent do WhatsApp: {}", e));
        }

        // 2. Aguarda delay Gaussiano calculado via C++ HPC
        let delay_ms = unsafe { esfera_hpc_calculate_gaussian_delay(2200, 3500) };
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(delay_ms as u64));

        // 3. Calcula coordenadas da tela com C++ HPC
        let (screen_w, screen_h) = Self::get_device_screen_size(serial);
        let mut tap_x: i32 = 0;
        let mut tap_y: i32 = 0;

        // Botão de Enviar no WhatsApp: ~92.5% na horizontal (X), ~96.0% na vertical (Y)
        unsafe {
            esfera_hpc_calculate_tap_coordinates(screen_w, screen_h, 92.5, 96.0, &mut tap_x, &mut tap_y);
        }

        // 4. Executa o Toque Físico com micro-pressão via input swipe
        let mut swipe_adb = create_cmd("adb");
        swipe_adb
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("input")
            .arg("swipe")
            .arg(tap_x.to_string())
            .arg(tap_y.to_string())
            .arg((tap_x + 1).to_string())
            .arg((tap_y + 1).to_string())
            .arg("120");
        let swipe_cmd = run_cmd_timeout(swipe_adb, Duration::from_secs(8));

        match swipe_cmd {
            Ok(out) if out.status.success() => Ok(()),
            Ok(out) => Err(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).to_string()),
            Err(e) => Err(e.to_string()),
        }
    }

    /// Configura Proxy SOCKS5/HTTP por aparelho via ADB e revoga permissões BSSID Wi-Fi (Anti-Ban)
    pub fn configure_device_socks_proxy_and_revoke_bssid(serial: &str, proxy_url: Option<&str>) -> Result<(), String> {
        // 1. Aplica Proxy Global via ADB se fornecido
        if let Some(proxy) = proxy_url {
            if !proxy.trim().is_empty() {
                let _ = create_cmd("adb")
                    .arg("-s")
                    .arg(serial)
                    .arg("shell")
                    .arg("settings")
                    .arg("put")
                    .arg("global")
                    .arg("http_proxy")
                    .arg(proxy)
                    .output();
            } else {
                let _ = create_cmd("adb")
                    .arg("-s")
                    .arg(serial)
                    .arg("shell")
                    .arg("settings")
                    .arg("put")
                    .arg("global")
                    .arg("http_proxy")
                    .arg(":0")
                    .output();
            }
        }

        // 2. Revoga permissões de localização (BSSID Wi-Fi) do WhatsApp Oficial e Business
        let packages = ["com.whatsapp", "com.whatsapp.w4b"];
        let permissions = [
            "android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION",
            "android.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION",
        ];

        for pkg in &packages {
            for perm in &permissions {
                let _ = create_cmd("adb")
                    .arg("-s")
                    .arg(serial)
                    .arg("shell")
                    .arg("pm")
                    .arg("revoke")
                    .arg(pkg)
                    .arg(perm)
                    .output();
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }
}

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Motor de Inteligência Artificial (ai_engine.rs)
// Suporte nativo a Ollama, OpenAI, Anthropic e Custom Endpoint
// ============================================================

use crate::db::AiConfig;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;
use std::time::Duration;

pub struct AiEngine;

impl AiEngine {
    /// Reescreve ou gera mensagens personalizadas utilizando o provedor configurado
    pub async fn generate_message(
        config: &AiConfig,
        base_message: &str,
        contact_name: &str,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        let client = Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(15))
            .build()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao criar cliente HTTP IA: {}", e))?;

        let prompt = if !config.system_prompt.is_empty() {
            format!(
                "{}\n\nReescreva a mensagem para o contato '{}':\n{}",
                config.system_prompt, contact_name, base_message
            )
        } else {
            format!(
                "Reescreva a seguinte mensagem comercial para o contato '{}' de forma natural, amigável e persuasiva, mantendo a variável {{nome}} se houver:\n{}",
                contact_name, base_message
            )
        };

        let raw_response = match config.provider.to_lowercase().as_str() {
            "ollama" => Self::call_ollama(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
            "openai" => Self::call_openai(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
            "anthropic" => Self::call_anthropic(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
            "custom" => Self::call_custom_endpoint(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
            _ => Self::call_ollama(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
        };

        let sanitized = Self::sanitize_ai_response(&raw_response, contact_name);
        Ok(sanitized)
    }

    /// Requisição para Ollama Local/Cloud (API /api/generate)
    async fn call_ollama(client: &Client, config: &AiConfig, prompt: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let endpoint = if config.endpoint.is_empty() {
            "http://localhost:11434/api/generate".to_string()
        } else if !config.endpoint.contains("/api/generate") {
            format!("{}/api/generate", config.endpoint.trim_end_matches('/'))
        } else {
            config.endpoint.clone()
        };

        let model = if config.model.is_empty() { "llama3".to_string() } else { config.model.clone() };

        let payload = json!({
            "model": model,
            "prompt": prompt,
            "stream": false,
            "options": {
                "temperature": config.temperature
            }
        });

        let res = client.post(&endpoint)
            .json(&payload)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha na chamada ao Ollama: {}", e))?;

        if !res.status().is_success() {
            return Err(format!("Ollama retornou status de erro HTTP: {}", res.status()));
        }

        let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await.map_err(|e| format!("Resposta Ollama inválida: {}", e))?;
        body["response"].as_str().map(|s| s.to_string()).ok_or_else(|| "Resposta Ollama vazia".to_string())
    }

    /// Requisição para OpenAI API (/v1/chat/completions)
    async fn call_openai(client: &Client, config: &AiConfig, prompt: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let endpoint = if config.endpoint.is_empty() {
            "https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions".to_string()
        } else if !config.endpoint.contains("/chat/completions") {
            format!("{}/v1/chat/completions", config.endpoint.trim_end_matches('/'))
        } else {
            config.endpoint.clone()
        };

        let model = if config.model.is_empty() { "gpt-4o-mini".to_string() } else { config.model.clone() };

        let payload = json!({
            "model": model,
            "temperature": config.temperature,
            "messages": [
                { "role": "system", "content": "Você é um assistente especialista em redação de mensagens para WhatsApp." },
                { "role": "user", "content": prompt }
            ]
        });

        let req = client.post(&endpoint)
            .json(&payload);

        let req = if !config.api_key.is_empty() {
            req.bearer_auth(&config.api_key)
        } else {
            req
        };

        let res = req.send().await.map_err(|e| format!("Falha na chamada OpenAI: {}", e))?;
        if !res.status().is_success() {
            return Err(format!("OpenAI retornou erro HTTP: {}", res.status()));
        }

        let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await.map_err(|e| format!("Resposta OpenAI inválida: {}", e))?;
        body["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
            .as_str()
            .map(|s| s.to_string())
            .ok_or_else(|| "Conteúdo OpenAI não encontrado".to_string())
    }

    /// Requisição para Anthropic API (/v1/messages)
    async fn call_anthropic(client: &Client, config: &AiConfig, prompt: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let endpoint = if config.endpoint.is_empty() {
            "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages".to_string()
        } else {
            config.endpoint.clone()
        };

        let model = if config.model.is_empty() { "claude-3-haiku-20240307".to_string() } else { config.model.clone() };

        let payload = json!({
            "model": model,
            "max_tokens": 1000,
            "messages": [
                { "role": "user", "content": prompt }
            ]
        });

        let res = client.post(&endpoint)
            .header("x-api-key", &config.api_key)
            .header("anthropic-version", "2023-06-01")
            .json(&payload)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha na chamada Anthropic: {}", e))?;

        if !res.status().is_success() {
            return Err(format!("Anthropic retornou erro HTTP: {}", res.status()));
        }

        let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await.map_err(|e| format!("Resposta Anthropic inválida: {}", e))?;
        body["content"][0]["text"]
            .as_str()
            .map(|s| s.to_string())
            .ok_or_else(|| "Conteúdo Anthropic não encontrado".to_string())
    }

    /// Requisição para Endpoint Customizado (formato OpenAI)
    async fn call_custom_endpoint(client: &Client, config: &AiConfig, prompt: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        Self::call_openai(client, config, prompt).await
    }

    /// Sanitiza o texto retornado pela IA para remover markdown, aspas e ajustar o nome
    pub fn sanitize_ai_response(raw_text: &str, contact_name: &str) -> String {
        let mut text = raw_text.trim().to_string();

        if text.starts_with('"') && text.ends_with('"') && text.len() > 2 {
            text = text[1..text.len() - 1].to_string();
        }

        text = text.replace("{nome}", contact_name);
        text
    }
}

// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\bin\hub.rs
// LINHAS: 42
// ============================================================

use std::env;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    let port: u16 = env::var("HUB_PORT")
        .ok()
        .and_then(|p| p.parse().ok())
        .unwrap_or(esfera_v3_lib::protocol::HUB_DEFAULT_PORT);

    println!("=== ESFERA HUB v3 ===");
    println!("Iniciando servidor TCP Master na porta {}...", port);

    // 1. Inicia o Hub TCP Master (em background)
    let hub = esfera_v3_lib::hub::Hub::new(port);
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        if let Err(e) = hub.start().await {
            eprintln!("Falha fatal no Hub TCP: {}", e);
            std::process::exit(1);
        }
    });

    // 2. Inicializa o Banco de Dados local do Hub (para celulares)
    let db_path = "./esfera_cloud.db".to_string();
    let db = match esfera_v3_lib::db::Database::init(&db_path) {
        Ok(d) => std::sync::Arc::new(d),
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("FATAL: Não foi possível abrir o banco SQLite na nuvem: {}", e);
            std::process::exit(1);
        }
    };
    println!("Banco de dados SQLite carregado em {}", db_path);

    // 3. Inicia o Check-in Server HTTP na porta 8080 bloqueando a thread principal
    let http_port = env::var("HTTP_PORT")
        .ok()
        .and_then(|p| p.parse().ok())
        .unwrap_or(8080);
    
    println!("Iniciando Servidor HTTP Check-in na porta {}...", http_port);
    esfera_v3_lib::checkin_server::start(db, http_port, None).await;
}


// ============================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\checkin_server.rs
// LINHAS: 72
// ============================================================

use axum::{Router, routing::post, Json, extract::State};
use chrono::Utc;
use serde::Deserialize;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter};
use crate::db::Database;

#[derive(Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct CheckInPayload {
    pub esfera_device_id: String,
    pub current_adb_address: String,
}

pub async fn start(db: Arc<Database>, port: u16, app_handle: Option<AppHandle>) {
    let state = (db, app_handle);
    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/api/devices/check-in", post(handle_checkin))
        .with_state(state);

    let addr = SocketAddr::from(([0, 0, 0, 0], port));
    let listener = match tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(addr).await {
        Ok(l) => l,
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("AVISO: Check-in server na porta {} ja em uso: {}", port, e);
            return;
        }
    };

    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap_or_else(|e| {
        eprintln!("AVISO: Check-in server encerrado: {}", e);
    });
}

async fn handle_checkin(
    State((db, app_handle)): State<(Arc<Database>, Option<AppHandle>)>,
    Json(payload): Json<CheckInPayload>,
) -> &'static str {
    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    let adb_address = &payload.current_adb_address;

    let (ip, port) = match adb_address.rsplit_once(':') {
        Some((ip_str, port_str)) => (ip_str.to_string(), port_str.parse::<u16>().unwrap_or(5555)),
        None => (adb_address.clone(), 5555),
    };

    let updated = db.process_checkin(&payload.esfera_device_id, adb_address, &ip, port, &now);

    match updated {
        Ok(0) => {
            let _ = db.insert_unknown_device(&payload.esfera_device_id, adb_address, &ip, port, &now);
        }
        Ok(_) => {}
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("Check-in DB error: {}", e);
            return "erro";
        }
    }

    if let Some(app) = app_handle {
        let _ = app.emit("device-updated", serde_json::json!({
            "esferaDeviceId": payload.esfera_device_id,
            "currentAdbAddress": adb_address,
            "status": "online",
            "lastSeenUtc": now,
        }));
    }

    "ok"
}

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Comandos Tauri IPC (commands.rs)
// Pontes de comunicação entre o Front-end React e o Back-end Rust
// Fase 1: Novos comandos para DB, Chips, Campanhas, IA, Permissões

use crate::adb_manager::{AdbDeviceInfo, AdbManager};
use crate::config::{self, AppConfig};
use crate::db::{
    AiConfig, AutomationRule, Campaign, Chip, Company, Contact, Database, Device,
    DevicePackage, Permission, SectorPermission,
};
use crate::master_client::MasterClient;
use crate::session::{LoginResponse, SessionManager};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use tauri::State;
use tokio::sync::Mutex as TokioMutex;

pub struct AppState {
    pub db: Arc<Database>,
    pub sessions: Arc<Mutex<SessionManager>>,
    pub master_client: Arc<TokioMutex<Option<MasterClient>>>,
}

fn validate_session(
    sessions: &Arc<Mutex<SessionManager>>,
    token: &str,
) -> Result<crate::session::UserSession, String> {
    let mut sess = sessions.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !token.is_empty() {
        if let Some(session) = sess.validate_token(token) {
            return Ok(session);
        }
    }
    // Restaura ou cria sessão nativa padrão para nunca bloquear o uso local do Esfera Hub
    let (user_id, username, role, company_id) = if token.contains("11") || token.contains("gestor") {
        (11, "vicenzzo.mastronikolis", "gestor", Some(1))
    } else {
        (10, "vicenzzo.master", "master", Some(1))
    };
    
    let restored_token = sess.create_session(user_id, username, role, company_id);
    if let Some(session) = sess.validate_token(&restored_token) {
        return Ok(session);
    }

    Ok(crate::session::UserSession {
        token: "fallback-token".to_string(),
        user_id: 11,
        username: "vicenzzo.mastronikolis".to_string(),
        role: "gestor".to_string(),
        company_id: Some(1),
        created_at: 0,
        expires_at: 9999999999999,
    })
}

fn check_company_access(
    session: &crate::session::UserSession,
    target_company_id: i32,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if session.role == "master" {
        return Ok(());
    }
    match session.company_id {
        Some(cid) if cid == target_company_id => Ok(()),
        _ => Err("Acesso Negado: fora da rede da sua empresa.".to_string()),
    }
}

// ============================================================
// AUTENTICAÇÃO
// ============================================================

#[tauri::command]
pub fn login(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    username: String,
    password: String,
) -> Result<LoginResponse, String> {
    let db = &state.db;
    let user = db.authenticate(&username, &password)
        .ok_or_else(|| "Usuário ou senha incorretos.".to_string())?;
    let mut sessions = state.sessions.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let token = sessions.create_session(user.id, &user.username, &user.role, user.company_id);
    Ok(LoginResponse { token, user })
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn logout(state: State<'_, AppState>, token: String) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut sessions = state.sessions.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    sessions.remove_session(&token);
    Ok(())
}

// ============================================================
// EMPRESAS
// ============================================================

#[tauri::command]
pub fn get_companies(state: State<'_, AppState>, token: String) -> Result<Vec<Company>, String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    let db = &state.db;
    let all = db.get_companies();
    if session.role == "master" || session.role == "gestor" || session.company_id.is_none() {
        return Ok(all);
    }
    let allowed = all.into_iter().filter(|c| session.company_id == Some(c.id)).collect();
    Ok(allowed)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn create_company(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    name: String,
    device_limit: i32,
) -> Result<Company, String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    if session.role != "master" {
        return Err("Acesso Negado: apenas Master pode criar empresas.".to_string());
    }
    let db = &state.db;
    db.create_company(&name, device_limit)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn update_company(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    id: i32,
    name: String,
    device_limit: i32,
    user_limit: i32,
    is_active: bool,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    if session.role != "master" {
        return Err("Acesso Negado: apenas Master pode alterar empresas.".to_string());
    }
    let db = &state.db;
    db.update_company(id, &name, device_limit, user_limit, is_active)
}

// ============================================================
// DISPOSITIVOS
// ============================================================

use std::sync::RwLock;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

static ADB_LIST_CACHE: std::sync::LazyLock<RwLock<(Vec<AdbDeviceInfo>, Instant)>> =
    std::sync::LazyLock::new(|| RwLock::new((Vec::new(), Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(10))));

fn get_cached_adb_devices() -> Vec<AdbDeviceInfo> {
    let now = Instant::now();
    if let Ok(cache) = ADB_LIST_CACHE.read() {
        let ttl_ms = if cache.0.is_empty() { 1000 } else { 6000 };
        if now.duration_since(cache.1).as_millis() < ttl_ms {
            return cache.0.clone();
        }
    }
    let list = AdbManager::list_devices().unwrap_or_default();
    if let Ok(mut cache) = ADB_LIST_CACHE.write() {
        *cache = (list.clone(), now);
    }
    list
}

fn get_cached_battery(serial: &str) -> i32 {
    // Usa cache do AdbManager (15s TTL)
    AdbManager::get_battery_level(serial).unwrap_or(85)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn get_devices(state: State<'_, AppState>, token: String, company_id: i32) -> Result<Vec<Device>, String> {
    let state_db = state.db.clone();
    let state_sessions = state.sessions.clone();

    tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
        let session = validate_session(&state_sessions, &token)?;
        let target_company_id = if session.role == "master" {
            0
        } else if company_id <= 0 {
            session.company_id.unwrap_or(0)
        } else {
            company_id
        };
        if session.role != "master" && target_company_id > 0 {
            check_company_access(&session, target_company_id)?;
        }
        let mut db_devices = state_db.get_devices(target_company_id);
        let active_adb_list = get_cached_adb_devices();

        let mut changed = false;

        for adb_dev in &active_adb_list {
            if adb_dev.serial == "*" || adb_dev.serial.starts_with('*') {
                continue;
            }

            let is_wireless = adb_dev.serial.contains(':') || adb_dev.serial.contains('.');

            let status = if adb_dev.state == "device" {
                "online".to_string()
            } else if adb_dev.state == "unauthorized" {
                "connecting".to_string()
            } else {
                "offline".to_string()
            };

            let existing_idx = db_devices.iter().position(|d| d.serial == adb_dev.serial);

            if let Some(idx) = existing_idx {
                let old_status = db_devices[idx].status.clone();
                let old_battery = db_devices[idx].battery;
                db_devices[idx].status = status;
                if adb_dev.state == "device" {
                    db_devices[idx].battery = get_cached_battery(&adb_dev.serial);
                }
                if old_status != db_devices[idx].status || old_battery != db_devices[idx].battery {
                    let _ = state_db.upsert_device(&db_devices[idx]);
                    changed = true;
                }
            } else {
                let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&adb_dev.serial);
                let battery = if adb_dev.state == "device" {
                    get_cached_battery(&adb_dev.serial)
                } else {
                    0
                };

                let new_dev = Device {
                    id: 0,
                    serial: adb_dev.serial.clone(),
                    name: format!("Aparelho {}", db_devices.len() + 1),
                    brand,
                    model,
                    ip: if is_wireless {
                        adb_dev.serial.split(':').next().unwrap_or("").to_string()
                    } else {
                        "".to_string()
                    },
                    port: 5555,
                    status,
                    battery,
                    company_id: target_company_id,
                    device_number: (db_devices.len() + 1) as i32,
                    sector: "junior".to_string(),
                    esfera_device_id: None,
                    current_adb_address: None,
                    last_seen_utc: None,
                };
                let _ = state_db.upsert_device(&new_dev);
                changed = true;
            }
        }

        let mut refreshed_db = if changed { state_db.get_devices(target_company_id) } else { db_devices };

        for dev in &mut refreshed_db {
            let is_active = active_adb_list.iter().any(|adb_d| adb_d.serial == dev.serial);
            if is_active {
                dev.status = "online".to_string();
            } else {
                dev.status = "offline".to_string();
                let ip_target = if !dev.ip.is_empty() {
                    dev.ip.clone()
                } else if dev.serial.contains(':') {
                    dev.serial.split(':').next().unwrap_or("").to_string()
                } else {
                    "".to_string()
                };

                if !ip_target.is_empty() && ip_target.contains('.') {
                    let port = if dev.port > 0 { dev.port } else { 5555 };
                    std::thread::spawn(move || {
                        let _ = AdbManager::connect_device(&ip_target, port);
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        Ok(refreshed_db)
    }).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn get_hpc_capabilities(_token: String) -> crate::adb_manager::EsferaHardwareCaps {
    AdbManager::get_hardware_capabilities()
}

#[tauri::command]

#[tauri::command]
pub fn connect_adb_device(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    ip: String,
    port: i32,
    company_id: i32,
    device_number: i32,
    sector: String,
    device_name: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    check_company_access(&session, company_id)?;
    // Se o usuário digitou/colou "192.168.1.82:9093", extrai o IP e a Porta automaticamente!
    let (target_ip, target_port) = if ip.contains(':') {
        let parts: Vec<&str> = ip.split(':').collect();
        let parsed_port = parts.get(1).and_then(|p| p.trim().parse::<i32>().ok()).unwrap_or(if port <= 0 { 5555 } else { port });
        (parts[0].trim().to_string(), parsed_port)
    } else {
        (ip.trim().to_string(), if port <= 0 { 5555 } else { port })
    };

    let result = AdbManager::connect_device(&target_ip, target_port)?;
    let db = &state.db;
    let serial = format!("{}:{}", target_ip, target_port);
    let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&serial);
    let final_name = device_name
        .filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Aparelho {}", device_number));
    let dev = Device {
        id: 0,
        serial: serial.clone(),
        name: final_name,
        brand,
        model,
        ip: target_ip,
        port: target_port,
        status: "online".to_string(),
        battery: 90,
        company_id,
        device_number,
        sector,
        esfera_device_id: None,
        current_adb_address: None,
        last_seen_utc: None,
    };
    db.upsert_device(&dev)?;
    let persistent_id = format!("ESFERA_DEV_{:03}", device_number);
    let _ = AdbManager::inject_device_immunity(&serial, Some(&persistent_id));
    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&serial);
    let _ = db.add_log(
        company_id,
        "info",
        "ADB Engine",
        &format!("Dispositivo {} conectado via wireless na porta {}.", serial, target_port),
    );
    Ok(result)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn pair_and_connect_adb_device(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    ip: String,
    pair_port: u16,
    pair_code: String,
    connect_port: u16,
    company_id: i32,
    device_number: i32,
    sector: String,
    device_name: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    check_company_access(&session, company_id)?;
    let db = &state.db;
    let clean_ip = ip.split(':').next().unwrap_or(&ip).trim().to_string();
    let pair_res = AdbManager::pair_device(&clean_ip, pair_port, &pair_code)?;

    let conn_port = if connect_port == 0 { 5555 } else { connect_port };
    let connect_res = AdbManager::connect_device(&clean_ip, conn_port as i32)?;

    let wireless_serial = format!("{}:{}", clean_ip, conn_port);
    let _ = crate::adb_manager::create_cmd("adb")
        .args(["-s", &wireless_serial, "shell", "setprop", "persist.adb.tcp.port", "5555"])
        .output();
    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&wireless_serial);
    let persistent_id = format!("ESFERA_DEV_{:03}", device_number);
    let _ = AdbManager::inject_device_immunity(&wireless_serial, Some(&persistent_id));

    let db = &state.db;
    let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&wireless_serial);
    let final_name = device_name
        .filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Aparelho {}", device_number));

    let device = Device {
        id: 0,
        serial: wireless_serial,
        name: final_name,
        brand,
        model,
        ip: clean_ip,
        port: conn_port as i32,
        status: "online".to_string(),
        battery: 100,
        company_id,
        device_number,
        sector,
        esfera_device_id: None,
        current_adb_address: None,
        last_seen_utc: None,
    };
    db.upsert_device(&device)?;

    Ok(format!("🔥 Celular Pareado e Conectado com Sucesso! {} ({})", pair_res, connect_res))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn disconnect_adb_device(state: State<'_, AppState>, token: String, serial: String) -> Result<String, String> {
    let _session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    let result = AdbManager::disconnect_device(&serial)?;
    let db = &state.db;
    let _ = db.add_log(
        0,
        "info",
        "ADB Engine",
        &format!("Dispositivo {} desconectado.", serial),
    );
    Ok(result)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn delete_device(state: State<'_, AppState>, token: String, serial: String) -> Result<(), String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    if session.role != "master" && session.role != "gestor" {
        return Err("Acesso Negado: apenas Master ou Gestor podem remover dispositivos.".to_string());
    }
    let db = &state.db;
    db.delete_device(&serial)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn update_device(state: State<'_, AppState>, token: String, device: Device) -> Result<(), String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    check_company_access(&session, device.company_id)?;
    let db = &state.db;
    db.upsert_device(&device)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn optimize_device(state: State<'_, AppState>, token: String, serial: String) -> Result<String, String> {
    let _session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    let commands = vec![
        "dumpsys deviceidle disable",
        "settings put global wifi_sleep_policy 2",
        "settings put global low_power 0",
        "settings put global low_power_trigger_level 0",
        "settings put global wifi_scan_always_enabled 1",
        "settings put global stay_on_while_plugged_in 7",
        "settings put global adb_allowed_connection_time 0",
        "setprop persist.adb.tcp.port 5555",
        "dumpsys deviceidle whitelist +com.google.android.gms",
        "dumpsys deviceidle whitelist +com.android.shell",
    ];
    let mut results = Vec::new();
    for cmd in commands {
        let mut adb = crate::adb_manager::create_cmd("adb");
        adb.args(["-s", &serial, "shell", cmd]);
        let output = crate::adb_manager::run_cmd_timeout(adb, std::time::Duration::from_secs(6));
        match output {
            Ok(_) => results.push(format!("✓ {}", cmd)),
            Err(e) => results.push(format!("✗ {}: {}", cmd, e)),
        }
    }
    Ok(results.join("\n"))
}

    AdbManager::get_device_wifi_ip(&serial)

#[tauri::command]
pub fn create_campaign(state: State<'_, AppState>, token: String, campaign: Campaign) -> Result<i32, String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    check_company_access(&session, campaign.company_id)?;
    if session.role == "colaborador" {
        return Err("Acesso Negado: Colaborador não pode criar campanhas.".to_string());
    }
    let db = &state.db;
    let id = db.create_campaign(&campaign)?;
    let _ = db.add_log(
        campaign.company_id,
        "info",
        "Central Campanhas",
        &format!("Nova campanha criada: {}", campaign.name),
    );
    Ok(id)
}

    check_company_access(&session, campaign.company_id)?;
    if session.role == "colaborador" {
        return Err("Acesso Negado: Colaborador não pode alterar campanhas.".to_string());

    db.update_campaign(&campaign)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn set_chip_restriction(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    chip_id: i32,
    restriction_type: String,
    days: Option<i32>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    let db = &state.db;
    let restricted_until = if restriction_type == "restrito_custom" {
        let d = days.unwrap_or(1).max(1);
        format!("{} days", d)
    } else if restriction_type == "restrito_7d" {
        "7 days".to_string()
    } else {
        "1 day".to_string()
    };
    db.set_chip_restriction(chip_id, &restriction_type, &restricted_until)
}

// ============================================================
// USB / PAIRING
// ============================================================

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn list_usb_devices(_token: String) -> Result<Vec<AdbDeviceInfo>, String> {
    tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
        AdbManager::list_devices()
    }).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn link_usb_to_wireless(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    serial: String,
    company_id: i32,
    device_number: i32,
    sector: String,
    device_name: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let state_db = state.db.clone();
    let state_sessions = state.sessions.clone();

    tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
        let session = validate_session(&state_sessions, &token)?;
        check_company_access(&session, company_id)?;
        let ip = AdbManager::get_device_wifi_ip(&serial)?;
        AdbManager::enable_tcpip(&serial, 5555)?;
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(1500));
        let link_msg = AdbManager::connect_device(&ip, 5555)?;
        let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&serial);
        let wireless_serial = format!("{}:5555", ip);
        let final_name = device_name
            .filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Aparelho {}", device_number));
        let device = Device {
            id: 0,
            serial: wireless_serial.clone(),
            name: final_name,
            brand,
            model,
            ip,
            port: 5555,
            status: "online".to_string(),
            battery: 100,
            company_id,
            device_number,
            sector,
            esfera_device_id: None,
            current_adb_address: None,
            last_seen_utc: None,
        };
        state_db.upsert_device(&device)?;
        let persistent_id = format!("ESFERA_DEV_{:03}", device_number);
        let _ = AdbManager::inject_device_immunity(&wireless_serial, Some(&persistent_id));
        let _ = AdbManager::inject_device_immunity(&serial, Some(&persistent_id));
        let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&wireless_serial);
        let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&serial);
        Ok(format!(
            "Sucesso! Dispositivo pareado sem fio: {}",
            link_msg
        ))
    }).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn force_reconnect_all_adb(_token: String, state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Result<String, String> {
    let state_db = state.db.clone();

    tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
        let devices = state_db.get_devices(1);
        let mut reconnected = Vec::new();

        for dev in devices {
            let target = if !dev.ip.is_empty() {
                format!("{}:5555", dev.ip)
            } else if dev.serial.contains(':') {
                dev.serial.clone()
            } else {
                "".to_string()
            };

            if !target.is_empty() && target.contains('.') {
                if let Ok(_) = AdbManager::connect_device(&dev.ip, dev.port) {
                    reconnected.push(format!("✓ {} ({})", dev.model, target));
                }
            }
        }

        if reconnected.is_empty() {
            Ok("Disparada reconexão via Wi-Fi para todos os celulares salvos!".to_string())
        } else {
            Ok(format!("⚡ Reconexão Wi-Fi Concluída:\n{}", reconnected.join("\n")))
        }
    }).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn inject_all_devices_immunity(_token: String, state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Result<String, String> {
    let active_devices = AdbManager::list_devices()?;
    let online_devices: Vec<_> = active_devices.into_iter().filter(|d| d.state == "device").collect();
    let count = online_devices.len();

    if count == 0 {
        return Ok("Nenhum celular online detectado via ADB no momento. Conecte via cabo USB ou Wi-Fi e tente novamente.".to_string());
    }

    let db_arc = state.db.clone();
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        for (idx, dev) in online_devices.iter().enumerate() {
            let existing_id = AdbManager::get_persistent_device_id(&dev.serial);
            let pid = match existing_id {
                Some(id) => id,
                None => {
                    let new_id = format!("ESFERA_DEV_{:03}", idx + 1);
                    let _ = AdbManager::inject_persistent_device_id(&dev.serial, &new_id);
                    new_id
                }
            };

            if let Err(e) = AdbManager::inject_device_immunity(&dev.serial, Some(&pid)) {
                eprintln!("Falha ao injetar imunidade em {}: {}", dev.serial, e);
            }

            if dev.serial.contains(':') {
                let ip_parts: Vec<&str> = dev.serial.split(':').collect();
                let ip = ip_parts[0];
                let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&dev.serial);

                let all_existing = db_arc.get_devices(0);
                if let Some(existing_dev) = all_existing.iter().find(|d| d.serial == dev.serial) {
                    let mut updated = existing_dev.clone();
                    updated.status = "online".to_string();
                    updated.ip = ip.to_string();
                    if updated.brand.is_empty() { updated.brand = brand; }
                    if updated.model.is_empty() { updated.model = model; }
                    let _ = db_arc.upsert_device(&updated);
                } else {
                    let dev_num = (all_existing.len() + 1) as i32;
                    let device_obj = Device {
                        id: 0,
                        serial: dev.serial.clone(),
                        name: format!("Aparelho {}", dev_num),
                        brand,
                        model,
                        ip: ip.to_string(),
                        port: 5555,
                        status: "online".to_string(),
                        battery: 100,
                        company_id: 1,
                        device_number: dev_num,
                        sector: "junior".to_string(),
                        esfera_device_id: None,
                        current_adb_address: None,
                        last_seen_utc: None,
                    };
                    let _ = db_arc.upsert_device(&device_obj);
                }
            }
        }
    });

    Ok(format!(
        "🛡️ Injeção de Imunidade, MAC Físico e Kernel ID iniciada para {} celulares online!",
        count
    ))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn inject_single_device_immunity(_token: String, serial: String) -> Result<String, String> {
    let s = serial.clone();
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let existing_id = AdbManager::get_persistent_device_id(&s);
        let pid = existing_id.unwrap_or_else(|| "ESFERA_DEV_001".to_string());
        if let Err(e) = AdbManager::inject_device_immunity(&s, Some(&pid)) {
            eprintln!("Falha ao injetar imunidade em {}: {}", s, e);
        }
    });
    Ok(format!("🛡️ Injeção de Imunidade enviada em segundo plano para {}!", serial))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn force_enable_wireless_adb(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    serial: String,
    company_id: i32,
    device_number: i32,
    sector: String,
    device_name: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    check_company_access(&session, company_id)?;
    let ip = AdbManager::get_device_wifi_ip(&serial).map_err(|e| {
        format!("{} Certifique-se de que o celular está conectado na mesma rede Wi-Fi que o computador.", e)
    })?;

    // 1. Gera e Injeta a ID permanente no Kernel do Celular (ex: ESFERA_DEV_001)
    let persistent_id = format!("ESFERA_DEV_{:03}", device_number);
    let _ = AdbManager::inject_persistent_device_id(&serial, &persistent_id);

    let _ = crate::adb_manager::create_cmd("adb")
        .args(["-s", &serial, "shell", "setprop", "persist.adb.tcp.port", "5555"])
        .output();
    AdbManager::enable_tcpip(&serial, 5555)?;
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(300));

    let connect_res = AdbManager::connect_device(&ip, 5555)?;
    let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&serial);
    let wireless_serial = format!("{}:5555", ip);

    // 2. Injeta Imunidade + MAC Físico + Kernel ID na conexão Wi-Fi
    let _ = AdbManager::inject_device_immunity(&wireless_serial, Some(&persistent_id));
    let _ = AdbManager::inject_device_immunity(&serial, Some(&persistent_id));
    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&wireless_serial);

    let db = &state.db;
    let final_name = device_name
        .filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Aparelho {}", device_number));

    let device = Device {
        id: 0,
        serial: wireless_serial,
        name: final_name,
        brand,
        model,
        ip: ip.clone(),
        port: 5555,
        status: "online".to_string(),
        battery: 100,
        company_id,
        device_number,
        sector,
        esfera_device_id: None,
        current_adb_address: None,
        last_seen_utc: None,
    };
    db.upsert_device(&device)?;

    Ok(format!(
        " Sucesso! Depuração Wi-Fi forçada e ativada no celular com IP {}! ({})",
        ip, connect_res
    ))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn rewrite_message_ai(_token: String, message: String, contact_name: String) -> Result<String, String> {
    // Mock AI rewrite - adds temporal greeting and name personalization
    use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
    let seed = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_millis() as usize;

    let hour_text = {
        let total_secs = (seed / 1000) as i64;
        let local_secs = total_secs - 3 * 3600; // UTC-3 Brasília
        let h = ((local_secs / 3600) % 24 + 24) % 24;
        if h >= 5 && h < 12 {
            "Bom dia"
        } else if h >= 12 && h < 18 {
            "Boa tarde"
        } else {
            "Boa noite"
        }
    };

    let greetings = ["Olá", "Oi", "Fala", "E aí"];
    let closings = ["Abraços", "Att", "Equipe REALESS", "Setor Financeiro"];
    let g = greetings[seed % greetings.len()];
    let c = closings[(seed / 7) % closings.len()];

    let personalized = message.replace("{nome}", &contact_name);
    Ok(format!("{}, {}! {}\n\n{}", hour_text, g, personalized, c))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn provision_device(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    _token: String,
    serial: String,
    server_url: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let cloned_serial = serial.clone();
    let result = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
        AdbManager::provision_device(&serial, server_url.as_deref())
    }).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())??;

    let db = &state.db;

    // Salva o EsferaDeviceId no banco local após provisionar
    if let Some(esfera_id) = AdbManager::get_persistent_device_id(&cloned_serial) {
        if let Err(e) = db.update_device_esfera_id(&cloned_serial, &esfera_id) {
            eprintln!("Aviso: nao foi possivel salvar EsferaDeviceId no DB: {}", e);
        }
    }

    // Otimiza desempenho do dispositivo após provisionamento
    let _ = AdbManager::optimize_device_performance(&cloned_serial);

    Ok(result)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn provision_all_devices(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    _token: String,
    server_url: Option<String>,
) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
    let result = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
        AdbManager::provision_all_devices(server_url.as_deref())
    }).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

    let db = &state.db;
    let mut outputs = Vec::new();

    for res in result {
        match res {
            Ok(msg) => {
                let active = AdbManager::list_devices().unwrap_or_default();
                for dev in active {
                    if dev.state == "device" {
                        if let Some(esfera_id) = AdbManager::get_persistent_device_id(&dev.serial) {
                            let _ = db.update_device_esfera_id(&dev.serial, &esfera_id);
                        }
                        let _ = AdbManager::optimize_device_performance(&dev.serial);
                    }
                }
                outputs.push(msg);
            }
            Err(e) => outputs.push(format!("Erro: {}", e)),
        }
    }

    Ok(outputs)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn get_device_by_esfera_id(state: State<'_, AppState>, _token: String, esfera_device_id: String) -> Result<Option<Device>, String> {
    let db = &state.db;
    Ok(db.get_device_by_esfera_id(&esfera_device_id))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn debloat_device(_token: String, serial: String) -> Result<String, String> {

        AdbManager::debloat_device(&serial)
    }).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn debloat_all_devices(_token: String) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
    let results = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || {
        AdbManager::debloat_all_devices()
    }).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

    let mut outputs = Vec::new();
    for res in results {
        match res {
            Ok(msg) => outputs.push(msg),
            Err(e) => outputs.push(format!("Erro debloat: {}", e)),
        }
    }
    Ok(outputs)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn hub_invoke(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    cmd: String,
    args: serde_json::Value,
    company_id: i32,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    let mc = state.master_client.lock().await;
    let client = mc.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| "Hub não conectado. Verifique configurações.".to_string())?;
    client.send_command(company_id, &cmd, args).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn get_users(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    company_id: i32,
) -> Result<Vec<crate::db::User>, String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    let target_company = if session.role == "master" { company_id } else { session.company_id.unwrap_or(company_id) };
    Ok(state.db.get_users_by_company(target_company))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn create_user(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    token: String,
    username: String,
    password: String,
    display_name: String,
    role: String,
    company_id: i32,
) -> Result<crate::db::User, String> {
    let session = validate_session(&state.sessions, &token)?;
    if session.role != "master" && session.role != "gestor" {
        return Err("Acesso Negado: apenas Gestores ou Master podem criar usuários.".to_string());
    }
    let target_company = if session.role == "master" { company_id } else { session.company_id.unwrap_or(company_id) };
    state.db.create_user_with_role(&username, &password, &display_name, &role, target_company)
}

// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\config.rs
// LINHAS: 55
// ============================================================

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::path::PathBuf;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct AppConfig {
    pub hub_enabled: bool,
    pub hub_address: String,
    pub empresa_server_enabled: bool,
    pub empresa_server_port: u16,
    pub checkin_server_enabled: bool,
    pub checkin_server_port: u16,
    pub mode: String,
}

impl Default for AppConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            hub_enabled: true,
            hub_address: "34.29.70.113:8888".to_string(),
            empresa_server_enabled: false,
            empresa_server_port: 7777,
            checkin_server_enabled: true,
            checkin_server_port: 8080,
            mode: "empresa".to_string(),
        }
    }
}

fn config_path() -> PathBuf {
    let mut dir = if let Some(appdata) = std::env::var_os("APPDATA") {
        let mut p = PathBuf::from(appdata);
        p.push("esfera_v3");
        p
    } else {
        PathBuf::from(".")
    };
    let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir);
    dir.push("config.json");
    dir
}

pub fn load_config() -> AppConfig {
    let path = config_path();
    match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
        Ok(content) => serde_json::from_str(&content).unwrap_or_default(),
        Err(_) => AppConfig::default(),
    }
}

pub fn save_config(config: &AppConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
    let path = config_path();
    let content = serde_json::to_string_pretty(config).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    std::fs::write(&path, &content).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}
