// ============================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Motor de Disparos em Background (dispatcher.rs)
// DispatchCoordinator & MobileWorker com Delay Gaussiano C++ HPC
// ============================================================

use crate::adb_manager::AdbManager;
use crate::ai_engine::AiEngine;
use crate::db::Database;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Emitter};
use tokio::task::spawn_blocking;
use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CampaignProgressEvent {
    pub campaign_id: i32,
    pub campaign_name: String,
    pub sent_count: i32,
    pub total_contacts: i32,
    pub last_contact_phone: String,
    pub delay_used_ms: i32,
    pub status: String,
}

pub struct Dispatcher;

impl Dispatcher {
    /// Inicia o worker assíncrono do motor de disparos em background com suporte a AppHandle
    pub fn start_worker(db_arc: Arc<Database>, app_handle: Option<AppHandle>) {
        tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
            loop {
                sleep(Duration::from_secs(3)).await;

                let db = db_arc.clone();
                let active_campaigns = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_running_campaigns().unwrap_or_default())
                    .await
                    .unwrap_or_default();

                for campaign in active_campaigns {
                    let db = db_arc.clone();
                    let next_contact = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_next_pending_contact(campaign.id))
                        .await
                        .unwrap_or(None);

                    if let Some(contact) = next_contact {
                        let db = db_arc.clone();
                        let phone_check = contact.phone.clone();
                        let already_sent = spawn_blocking(move || db.was_already_sent(&phone_check, campaign.id))
                            .await
                            .unwrap_or(false);

                        if already_sent {
                            let db = db_arc.clone();
                            let cid = contact.id;
                            let _ = spawn_blocking(move || { let _ = db.update_contact_status(cid, "already_sent"); }).await;
                            continue;
                        }

                        let message_to_send = if campaign.use_ai {
                            let db = db_arc.clone();
                            let ai_config = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_ai_config(campaign.company_id))
                                .await
                                .unwrap_or(None);

                            if let Some(config) = ai_config {
                                match AiEngine::generate_message(&config, &campaign.message, &contact.contact_name).await {
                                    Ok(custom_msg) => custom_msg,
                                    Err(_) => campaign.message.replace("{nome}", &contact.contact_name),
                                }
                            } else {
                                campaign.message.replace("{nome}", &contact.contact_name)
                            }
                        } else {
                            campaign.message.replace("{nome}", &contact.contact_name)
                        };

                        let db = db_arc.clone();
                        let active_devices = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_devices(campaign.company_id))
                            .await
                            .unwrap_or_default();
                        let online_devices: Vec<_> = active_devices.into_iter().filter(|d| d.status == "online").collect();

                        if let Some(target_dev) = online_devices.first() {
                            let serial = target_dev.serial.clone();
                            let msg = message_to_send.clone();
                            let phone = contact.phone.clone();
                            let _ = spawn_blocking(move || {
                                let _ = AdbManager::configure_device_socks_proxy_and_revoke_bssid(&serial, None);
                                let _ = AdbManager::send_whatsapp_intent_and_bezier_tap(&serial, &phone, &msg);
                            }).await;
                        }

                        let delay_min_ms = campaign.delay_min * 1000;
                        let delay_max_ms = campaign.delay_max * 1000;
                        let gaussian_delay_ms = AdbManager::calculate_gaussian_delay(delay_min_ms, delay_max_ms);

                        sleep(Duration::from_millis(gaussian_delay_ms as u64)).await;

                        let db = db_arc.clone();
                        let phone = contact.phone.clone();
                        let cid = campaign.id;
                        let cid2 = campaign.id;
                        let sent_before = campaign.sent_count;
                        let _ = spawn_blocking(move || {
                            let _ = db.mark_as_sent(&phone, cid);
                            let _ = db.update_contact_status(contact.id, "sent");
                            let _ = db.increment_campaign_sent_count(cid2);
                        }).await;

                        if let Some(ref handle) = app_handle {
                            let progress = CampaignProgressEvent {
                                campaign_id: campaign.id,
                                campaign_name: campaign.name.clone(),
                                sent_count: sent_before + 1,
                                total_contacts: campaign.contacts_count,
                                last_contact_phone: contact.phone.clone(),
                                delay_used_ms: gaussian_delay_ms,
                                status: "running".to_string(),
                            };
                            let _ = handle.emit("campaign-progress", progress);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    pub fn start_persistent_adb_keepalive_loop(db_arc: Arc<Database>) {
        tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
            loop {
                sleep(Duration::from_secs(45)).await;

                let db = db_arc.clone();
                let saved_devices = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_all_devices())
                    .await
                    .unwrap_or_default();

                for dev in saved_devices {
                    let ip_target = if !dev.ip.is_empty() {
                        dev.ip.clone()
                    } else if dev.serial.contains(':') {
                        dev.serial.split(':').next().unwrap_or("").to_string()
                    } else {
                        "".to_string()
                    };

                    if !ip_target.is_empty() && ip_target.contains('.') {
                        let port = if dev.port > 0 { dev.port } else { 5555 };
                        let ip = ip_target.clone();
                        if let Ok(_) = spawn_blocking(move || AdbManager::connect_device(&ip, port)).await.unwrap_or(Err("failed".to_string())) {
                            let full_serial = format!("{}:{}", ip_target, port);
                            let _ = spawn_blocking(move || AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&full_serial)).await;
                        }
                    } else if !dev.serial.is_empty() && !dev.serial.contains(':') {
                        let s = dev.serial.clone();
                        let _ = spawn_blocking(move || AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&s)).await;
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }
}

// ============================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\empresa_server.rs
// LINHAS: 109
// ============================================================

use crate::db::Database;
use crate::protocol::*;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncWriteExt, BufReader, BufWriter};
use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use tokio::task::spawn_blocking;
use tokio::time::{timeout, Duration};

pub struct EmpresaServer;

impl EmpresaServer {
    pub async fn start(db: Arc<Database>, port: u16) -> Result<(), String> {
        let listener = TcpListener::bind(format!("0.0.0.0:{}", port))
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao iniciar Empresa Server na porta {}: {}", port, e))?;
        println!("Empresa Server ouvindo na porta {} (LAN)", port);

        loop {
            let (stream, addr) = listener.accept().await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let db = db.clone();

            tokio::spawn(async move {
                if let Err(e) = handle_lan_client(db, stream, addr).await {
                    eprintln!("Erro no cliente LAN {}: {}", addr, e);
                }
            });
        }
    }
}

async fn handle_lan_client(
    db: Arc<Database>,
    stream: tokio::net::TcpStream,
    addr: std::net::SocketAddr,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let (reader, writer) = stream.into_split();
    let mut writer = BufWriter::new(writer);
    let mut reader = BufReader::new(reader);
    let mut line = String::new();

    loop {
        line.clear();
        match timeout(Duration::from_secs(300), reader.read_line(&mut line)).await {
            Ok(Ok(0)) | Ok(Err(_)) | Err(_) => break,
            Ok(Ok(_)) => {
                let trimmed = line.trim();
                if trimmed.is_empty() { continue; }
                if let Ok(msg) = deserialize_msg(trimmed.as_bytes()) {
                    match msg {
                        HubMessage::Command { cmd, args, id, .. } => {
                            let result = execute_command_locally(&db, &cmd, &args).await;
                            let response = match result {
                                Ok(val) => HubMessage::CommandResponse { id, result: Some(val), error: None },
                                Err(e) => HubMessage::CommandResponse { id, result: None, error: Some(e) },
                            };
                            let data = serialize_msg(&response)?;
                            writer.write_all(&data).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                            writer.flush().await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                        }
                        HubMessage::Ping => {
                            let data = serialize_msg(&HubMessage::Pong)?;
                            writer.write_all(&data).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                            writer.flush().await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                        }
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }
            }
            Err(_) => break,
        }
    }

    println!("Cliente LAN desconectado: {}", addr);
    Ok(())
}

async fn execute_command_locally(
    db: &Arc<Database>,
    cmd: &str,
    args: &serde_json::Value,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    match cmd {
        "get_companies" => {
            let db = db.clone();
            let companies = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_companies()).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            serde_json::to_value(companies).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
        }
        "get_devices" => {
            let company_id = args.get("companyId").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0) as i32;
            let db = db.clone();
            let devices = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_devices(company_id)).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            serde_json::to_value(devices).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
        }
        "get_campaigns" => {
            let company_id = args.get("companyId").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0) as i32;
            let db = db.clone();
            let campaigns = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_campaigns(company_id)).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            serde_json::to_value(campaigns).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
        }
        "get_dashboard_kpis" => {
            let company_id = args.get("companyId").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0) as i32;
            let db = db.clone();
            let kpis = spawn_blocking(move || db.get_dashboard_kpis(company_id)).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            serde_json::to_value(kpis).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
        }
        _ => Err(format!("Comando '{}' não disponível via Empresa Server", cmd)),
    }
}


// ============================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\hub.rs
// LINHAS: 172
// ============================================================

use crate::protocol::*;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncWriteExt, BufReader, BufWriter};
use tokio::net::tcp::OwnedWriteHalf;
use tokio::net::{TcpListener, TcpStream};
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use tokio::time::timeout;

struct HubConnection {
    writer: Arc<Mutex<BufWriter<OwnedWriteHalf>>>,
    role: String,
    company_id: Option<i32>,
    company_name: Option<String>,
}

pub struct Hub {
    port: u16,
    connections: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, HubConnection>>>,
    pending: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, String>>>, // cmd_id → master_session_id
}

impl Hub {
    pub fn new(port: u16) -> Self {
        Self { port, connections: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new())), pending: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new())) }
    }

    pub async fn start(self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let listener = TcpListener::bind(format!("0.0.0.0:{}", self.port))
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao iniciar Hub na porta {}: {}", self.port, e))?;
        println!("Hub iniciado na porta {}", self.port);

        loop {
            let (stream, addr) = listener.accept().await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let connections = self.connections.clone();
            let pending = self.pending.clone();
            tokio::spawn(async move {
                if let Err(e) = handle_connection(connections, pending, stream, addr).await {
                    eprintln!("Erro na conexão {}: {}", addr, e);
                }
            });
        }
    }
}

async fn send_raw(
    writer: &Mutex<BufWriter<OwnedWriteHalf>>,
    msg: &HubMessage,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let data = serialize_msg(msg)?;
    let mut w = writer.lock().await;
    w.write_all(&data).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    w.flush().await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}

async fn handle_connection(
    connections: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, HubConnection>>>,
    pending: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, String>>>,
    stream: TcpStream,
    addr: std::net::SocketAddr,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let (reader, writer) = stream.into_split();
    let writer = BufWriter::new(writer);
    let writer_lock = Arc::new(Mutex::new(writer));
    let mut reader = BufReader::new(reader);
    let mut line = String::new();
    let session_id = format!("sess_{}_{}", addr.port(), SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_millis());
    let mut authenticated = false;

    loop {
        line.clear();
        let n = match timeout(Duration::from_secs(300), reader.read_line(&mut line)).await {
            Ok(Ok(n)) => n,
            Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(e.to_string()),
            Err(_) => return Err("Timeout de leitura no Hub".to_string()),
        };
        if n == 0 {
            break;
        }
        let msg: HubMessage = deserialize_msg(line.trim().as_bytes())?;

        match msg {
            HubMessage::Auth { username, password, role } => {
                let auth_ok = role == "master" && username == AUTH_MASTER_USER && password == AUTH_MASTER_PASS;
                if auth_ok {
                    authenticated = true;
                    let conn = HubConnection {
                        writer: writer_lock.clone(),
                        role: role.clone(),
                        company_id: None,
                        company_name: None,
                    };
                    connections.lock().await.insert(session_id.clone(), conn);
                    let resp = HubMessage::AuthResponse { success: true, session_id: Some(session_id.clone()), error: None };
                    send_raw(&writer_lock, &resp).await?;
                    println!("{} '{}' autenticado no Hub", role, username);
                } else {
                    let resp = HubMessage::AuthResponse { success: false, session_id: None, error: Some("Credenciais inválidas.".to_string()) };
                    send_raw(&writer_lock, &resp).await?;
                }
            }
            HubMessage::Register { company_id, company_name } => {
                if !authenticated { continue; }
                let mut conns = connections.lock().await;
                if let Some(conn) = conns.get_mut(&session_id) {
                    conn.company_id = Some(company_id);
                    conn.company_name = Some(company_name.clone());
                }
                drop(conns);
                println!("Empresa '{}' (ID {}) registrada", company_name, company_id);
                let resp = HubMessage::RegisterResponse { success: true, error: None };
                send_raw(&writer_lock, &resp).await?;
            }
            HubMessage::Command { from, to_company, cmd, args, id } => {
                if !authenticated { continue; }
                let conns = connections.lock().await;
                let target = conns.iter().find(|(_, c)| {
                    c.role == "empresa" && c.company_id == to_company
                }).map(|(sid, _)| sid.clone());
                if let Some(target_sid) = target {
                    pending.lock().await.insert(id.clone(), session_id.clone());
                    let relay = HubMessage::Command { from, to_company, cmd, args, id: id.clone() };
                    if let Some(target_conn) = conns.get(&target_sid) {
                        send_raw(&target_conn.writer, &relay).await?;
                    }
                } else {
                    let resp = HubMessage::CommandResponse { id, result: None, error: Some("Empresa não conectada ao Hub.".to_string()) };
                    send_raw(&writer_lock, &resp).await?;
                }
            }
            HubMessage::CommandResponse { id, result, error } => {
                if !authenticated { continue; }
                let master_sid = {
                    let mut pend = pending.lock().await;
                    pend.remove(&id)
                };
                if let Some(master_sid) = master_sid {
                    let conns = connections.lock().await;
                    if let Some(master_conn) = conns.get(&master_sid) {
                        let resp = HubMessage::CommandResponse { id, result, error };
                        send_raw(&master_conn.writer, &resp).await?;
                    }
                }
            }
            HubMessage::EmpresaList { .. } => {
                if !authenticated { continue; }
                let conns = connections.lock().await;
                let empresas: Vec<EmpresaInfo> = conns.values()
                    .filter(|c| c.role == "empresa" && c.company_id.is_some())
                    .map(|c| EmpresaInfo {
                        company_id: c.company_id.unwrap(),
                        company_name: c.company_name.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
                        connected_since: SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_millis() as i64,
                    })
                    .collect();
                let resp = HubMessage::EmpresaList { empresas };
                send_raw(&writer_lock, &resp).await?;
            }
            HubMessage::Ping => {
                send_raw(&writer_lock, &HubMessage::Pong).await?;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    connections.lock().await.remove(&session_id);
    println!("Conexão {} encerrada", addr);
    Ok(())
}


// ============================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\hub_client.rs
// LINHAS: 155
// ============================================================

use crate::protocol::*;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncWriteExt, BufReader};
use tokio::net::tcp::{OwnedReadHalf, OwnedWriteHalf};
use tokio::net::TcpStream;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use tokio::time::{timeout, Duration};

pub struct HubClient {
    writer: Mutex<tokio::io::BufWriter<OwnedWriteHalf>>,
    reader: Mutex<BufReader<OwnedReadHalf>>,
    session_id: Option<String>,
}

impl HubClient {
    pub async fn connect(
        hub_addr: &str,
        company_id: i32,
        company_name: &str,
    ) -> Result<Self, String> {
        let stream = TcpStream::connect(hub_addr)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao conectar ao Hub {}: {}", hub_addr, e))?;

        let (reader, writer) = stream.into_split();
        let writer = tokio::io::BufWriter::new(writer);
        let reader = BufReader::new(reader);

        let mut client = Self {
            writer: Mutex::new(writer),
            reader: Mutex::new(reader),
            session_id: None,
        };

        client.authenticate("empresa").await?;
        client.register(company_id, company_name).await?;

        println!("Conectado ao Hub {} como empresa '{}' (ID {})", hub_addr, company_name, company_id);
        Ok(client)
    }

    async fn authenticate(&mut self, role: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let msg = HubMessage::Auth {
            username: AUTH_MASTER_USER.to_string(),
            password: AUTH_MASTER_PASS.to_string(),
            role: role.to_string(),
        };
        self.send(&msg).await?;

        let response = self.read_one().await?;
        match response {
            HubMessage::AuthResponse { success: true, session_id, .. } => {
                self.session_id = session_id;
                Ok(())
            }
            HubMessage::AuthResponse { success: false, error, .. } => {
                Err(error.unwrap_or_else(|| "Autenticação falhou.".to_string()))
            }
            _ => Err("Resposta de autenticação inválida.".to_string()),
        }
    }

    async fn register(&mut self, company_id: i32, company_name: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let msg = HubMessage::Register {
            company_id,
            company_name: company_name.to_string(),
        };
        self.send(&msg).await?;
        let response = self.read_one().await?;
        match response {
            HubMessage::RegisterResponse { success: true, .. } => Ok(()),
            HubMessage::RegisterResponse { success: false, error, .. } => {
                Err(error.unwrap_or_else(|| "Registro falhou.".to_string()))
            }
            _ => Err("Resposta de registro inválida.".to_string()),
        }
    }

    pub async fn send(&self, msg: &HubMessage) -> Result<(), String> {
        let data = serialize_msg(msg)?;
        let mut writer = self.writer.lock().await;
        writer.write_all(&data).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        writer.flush().await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    }

    pub async fn read_one(&self) -> Result<HubMessage, String> {
        let mut reader = self.reader.lock().await;
        let mut line = String::new();
        match timeout(Duration::from_secs(300), reader.read_line(&mut line)).await {
            Ok(Ok(_)) => {}
            Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(e.to_string()),
            Err(_) => return Err("Timeout de leitura no HubClient".to_string()),
        }
        if line.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err("Conexão fechada.".to_string());
        }
        deserialize_msg(line.trim().as_bytes())
    }

    pub async fn send_command(
        &self,
        cmd: &str,
        args: serde_json::Value,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
        let msg = HubMessage::Command {
            from: "empresa".to_string(),
            to_company: None,
            cmd: cmd.to_string(),
            args,
            id: format!("cmd_{}", std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .as_millis()),
        };
        self.send(&msg).await?;
        let response = self.read_one().await?;
        match response {
            HubMessage::CommandResponse { result: Some(val), .. } => Ok(val),
            HubMessage::CommandResponse { error: Some(e), .. } => Err(e),
            _ => Err("Resposta de comando inválida.".to_string()),
        }
    }

    pub async fn listen<F>(&self, on_command: F) -> Result<(), String>
    where
        F: Fn(String, serde_json::Value, String) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String>,
    {
        loop {
            let msg = self.read_one().await?;
            match msg {
                HubMessage::Command { from: _, cmd, args, id, .. } => {
                    let result = on_command(cmd, args, id.clone());
                    match result {
                        Ok(val) => {
                            let resp = HubMessage::CommandResponse {
                                id, result: Some(val), error: None,
                            };
                            self.send(&resp).await?;
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            let resp = HubMessage::CommandResponse {
                                id, result: None, error: Some(e),
                            };
                            self.send(&resp).await?;
                        }
                    }
                }
                HubMessage::Ping => {
                    self.send(&HubMessage::Pong).await?;
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }
}

mod adb_manager;

use commands::*;
use db::Database;
use crate::protocol::HubMessage;
use master_client::MasterClient;
use session::SessionManager;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::sync::Mutex as TokioMutex;

fn get_db_path() -> String {
    if let Some(mut dir) = std::env::var_os("APPDATA").map(PathBuf::from) {
        dir.push("esfera_v3");
        if let Err(e) = std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir) {
            eprintln!("Aviso: nao foi possivel criar diretorio do banco: {}", e);
        }
        dir.push("esfera_v3.db");
        return dir.to_string_lossy().to_string();
    }
    "esfera_v3.db".to_string()
}

#[cfg_attr(mobile, tauri::mobile_entry_point)]
pub fn run() {
    let db_path = get_db_path();
    let db = match Database::init(&db_path) {
        Ok(d) => d,
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("FATAL: Nao foi possivel abrir o banco SQLite: {}", e);
            std::process::exit(1);
        }
    };
    let db_arc = Arc::new(db);

    let db_arc_clone = Arc::clone(&db_arc);
    let sessions = Arc::new(Mutex::new(SessionManager::new()));
    let master_client: Arc<TokioMutex<Option<MasterClient>>> = Arc::new(TokioMutex::new(None));

    let app_config = config::load_config();

    // Runtime de fundo único para servidores e clients (LAN, Hub, Master)
    let bg_runtime = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().expect("Falha ao criar background runtime");

    if app_config.empresa_server_enabled {
        let db_for_server = db_arc.clone();
        let port = app_config.empresa_server_port;
        bg_runtime.spawn(async move {
            if let Err(e) = empresa_server::EmpresaServer::start(db_for_server, port).await {
                eprintln!("Empresa Server error: {}", e);
            }
        });
        println!("Empresa Server iniciado na porta {}", port);
    }

    // Empresa mode: connect to Hub with auto-reconnect
    if app_config.mode == "empresa" && app_config.hub_enabled && !app_config.hub_address.is_empty() {
        let db_for_hub = db_arc.clone();
        let hub_addr = app_config.hub_address.clone();
        bg_runtime.spawn(async move {
            let company_id = db_for_hub.get_companies().first().map(|c| c.id).unwrap_or(1);
            let company_name = format!("Empresa_{}", company_id);
            let mut backoff = 1;
            loop {
                match hub_client::HubClient::connect(&hub_addr, company_id, &company_name).await {
                    Ok(client) => {
                        println!("Conectado ao Hub {} como empresa '{}'", hub_addr, company_name);
                        backoff = 1;
                        let _ = client.listen(|cmd, args, _id| {
                            let db = db_for_hub.clone();
                            let res = match cmd.as_str() {
                                "get_companies" => serde_json::to_value(db.get_companies()).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
                                "get_devices" => {
                                    let cid = args.get("companyId").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0) as i32;
                                    serde_json::to_value(db.get_devices(cid)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
                                }
                                "get_campaigns" => {
                                    let cid = args.get("companyId").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0) as i32;
                                    serde_json::to_value(db.get_campaigns(cid)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
                                }
                                "get_dashboard_kpis" => {
                                    let cid = args.get("companyId").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0) as i32;
                                    serde_json::to_value(db.get_dashboard_kpis(cid)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
                                }
                                _ => Err(format!("Comando remoto '{}' não implementado", cmd)),
                            };
                            // Offload sync DB calls via std thread para não bloquear runtime async
                            std::thread::spawn(move || res).join().unwrap_or(Err("thread panicked".to_string()))
                        }).await;
                        eprintln!("Conexão com Hub perdida. Reconectando em {}s...", backoff);
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        eprintln!("Falha ao conectar ao Hub (tentativa em {}s): {}", backoff, e);
                    }
                }
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(backoff)).await;
                backoff = (backoff * 2).min(60);
            }
        });
    }

    // Master mode: connect MasterClient (stored for hub_invoke commands)
    if app_config.mode == "master" && app_config.hub_enabled && !app_config.hub_address.is_empty() {
        let hub_addr = app_config.hub_address.clone();
        let mc = master_client.clone();
        bg_runtime.spawn(async move {
            let mut backoff = 1;
            loop {
                match MasterClient::connect(&hub_addr).await {
                    Ok(client) => {
                        println!("Conectado ao Hub {} como Master", hub_addr);
                        *mc.lock().await = Some(client);
                        backoff = 1;
                        loop {
                            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(10)).await;
                            let mut guard = mc.lock().await;
                            if guard.is_none() { break; }
                            if let Some(ref c) = *guard {
                                if c.send_raw(&HubMessage::Ping).await.is_err() {
                                    *guard = None;
                                    break;
                                }
                                // Consume Pong
                                let _ = tokio::time::timeout(
                                    Duration::from_secs(3),
                                    c.read_one(),
                                ).await;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        eprintln!("Falha Master ao conectar ao Hub (tentativa em {}s): {}", backoff, e);
                    }
                }
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(backoff)).await;
                backoff = (backoff * 2).min(60);
            }
        });
        println!("Master Client conectando a {}...", app_config.hub_address);
    }

    let result = tauri::Builder::default()
        .plugin(tauri_plugin_opener::init())
        .setup(move |app| {
            let caps = adb_manager::AdbManager::get_hardware_capabilities();
            let hwid = adb_manager::AdbManager::get_hardware_uuid();
            println!("[Esfera v3 HPC Engine] HWID: {}", hwid);
            println!("[Esfera v3 HPC Engine] CPU Hardware Dispatch -> AVX2: {}, AVX-512: {}, SSE4.2: {}", caps.supports_avx2, caps.supports_avx512, caps.supports_sse42);

            let handle = app.handle().clone();
            if app_config.checkin_server_enabled {
                let db = db_arc_clone.clone();
                let port = app_config.checkin_server_port;
                tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
                    checkin_server::start(db, port, Some(handle)).await;
                });
            }

            dispatcher::Dispatcher::start_worker(db_arc_clone.clone(), Some(app.handle().clone()));
            dispatcher::Dispatcher::start_persistent_adb_keepalive_loop(db_arc_clone.clone());
            adb_manager::AdbManager::start_status_monitor(db_arc_clone);
            Ok(())
        })
        .manage(AppState { db: db_arc, sessions, master_client })
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![
            // Auth
            login,
            logout,
            // Empresas
            get_companies,
            create_company,
            update_company,
            // Dispositivos
            get_devices,
            connect_adb_device,
            disconnect_adb_device,
            delete_device,
            update_device,
            optimize_device,
            get_device_ip,
            // Campanhas
            get_campaigns,
            create_campaign,
            update_campaign,
            soft_delete_campaign,
            set_campaign_status,
            // Automações
            get_automations,
            create_automation,
            update_automation,
            soft_delete_automation,
            // Logs
            get_logs,
            add_log,
            clear_logs,
            // ADB Remoto
            capture_device_screen,
            send_adb_tap,
            send_adb_keyevent,
            get_hpc_capabilities,
            // Chips
            get_device_chips,
            add_device_chip,
            update_chip_status,
            set_chip_restriction,
            confirm_chip_release,
            move_chip,
            set_chip_holder,
            mark_chip_reserve,
            get_restricted_chips,
            // Contatos
            get_campaign_contacts,
            import_campaign_contacts,
            get_pending_contacts,
            update_contact_status,
            set_contact_custom_message,
            get_next_pending_contact,
            // Sent History (dedup)
            was_already_sent,
            // Device Status & Prefs
            is_device_banned,
            set_device_banned,
            get_device_prefs,
            set_device_prefs,
            // Device Packages
            get_device_package_statuses,
            update_device_package_status,
            // USB / Pairing
            list_usb_devices,
            link_usb_to_wireless,
            force_enable_wireless_adb,
            force_reconnect_all_adb,
            inject_all_devices_immunity,
            inject_single_device_immunity,
            pair_and_connect_adb_device,
            run_adb_diagnostic,
            restart_adb_server,
            scan_adb_subnet,
            deploy_adb_keepalive,
            get_hardware_uuid,
            launch_scrcpy_stream,
            launch_virtual_display_stream,
            send_intent_to_display,
            launch_camera_stream,
            inject_virtual_camera,
            inject_virtual_camera_region,
            inject_base64_frame,
            optimize_device_hpc,
            install_agent_apk,
            provision_device,
            provision_all_devices,
            debloat_device,
            debloat_all_devices,
            get_device_by_esfera_id,
            // AI Config
            get_ai_config,
            save_ai_config,
            // Permissions
            get_user_permissions,
            update_user_permission,
            get_sector_permissions,
            update_sector_permission,
            // Dashboard
            get_dashboard_kpis,
            // Campaign Dispatch
            send_campaign,
            pause_campaign,
            start_campaign,
            rewrite_message_ai,
            // Hub / Master
            hub_invoke,
            // Users & Roles
            get_users,
            create_user,
            // Device Assignments & Permissions
            assign_device_to_user,
            unassign_device_from_user,
            get_assigned_devices_for_user,
            request_device_access,
            get_device_permission_requests,
            approve_device_access,
            reject_device_access,
            end_device_access,
            get_devices_with_active_user_map,
            configure_device_proxy_and_antiban,
            trigger_manual_intent_bezier_dispatch,
            // Config
            get_app_config,
            save_app_config,
        ])
        .run(tauri::generate_context!());
    if let Err(e) = result {
        eprintln!("Erro ao iniciar aplicação Esfera v3: {}", e);
    }
}

// Prevents additional console window on Windows in release, DO NOT REMOVE!!
#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

#[tokio::main]

    esfera_v3_lib::run();

// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\master_client.rs
// LINHAS: 108
// ============================================================

use crate::protocol::*;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncWriteExt, BufReader};
use tokio::net::tcp::{OwnedReadHalf, OwnedWriteHalf};
use tokio::net::TcpStream;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use tokio::time::{timeout, Duration};

pub struct MasterClient {
    writer: Mutex<tokio::io::BufWriter<OwnedWriteHalf>>,
    reader: Mutex<BufReader<OwnedReadHalf>>,
    session_id: Option<String>,
}

impl MasterClient {
    pub async fn connect(hub_addr: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {
        let stream = TcpStream::connect(hub_addr)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao conectar ao Hub {}: {}", hub_addr, e))?;

        let (reader, writer) = stream.into_split();
        let writer = tokio::io::BufWriter::new(writer);
        let reader = BufReader::new(reader);

        let mut client = Self {
            writer: Mutex::new(writer),
            reader: Mutex::new(reader),
            session_id: None,
        };

        client.authenticate().await?;
        println!("Conectado ao Hub {} como Master", hub_addr);
        Ok(client)
    }

    async fn authenticate(&mut self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let msg = HubMessage::Auth {
            username: AUTH_MASTER_USER.to_string(),
            password: AUTH_MASTER_PASS.to_string(),
            role: "master".to_string(),
        };
        self.send_raw(&msg).await?;

        match self.read_one().await? {
            HubMessage::AuthResponse { success: true, session_id, .. } => {
                self.session_id = session_id;
                Ok(())
            }
            HubMessage::AuthResponse { success: false, error, .. } => {
                Err(error.unwrap_or_else(|| "Autenticação Master falhou.".to_string()))
            }
            _ => Err("Resposta inválida do Hub.".to_string()),
        }
    }

    pub async fn send_raw(&self, msg: &HubMessage) -> Result<(), String> {
        let data = serialize_msg(msg)?;
        let mut writer = self.writer.lock().await;
        writer.write_all(&data).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        writer.flush().await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    }

    pub async fn read_one(&self) -> Result<HubMessage, String> {
        let mut reader = self.reader.lock().await;
        let mut line = String::new();
        match timeout(Duration::from_secs(300), reader.read_line(&mut line)).await {
            Ok(Ok(_)) => {}
            Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(e.to_string()),
            Err(_) => return Err("Timeout de leitura no MasterClient".to_string()),
        }
        if line.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err("Conexão com Hub perdida.".to_string());
        }
        deserialize_msg(line.trim().as_bytes())
    }

    pub async fn get_empresas(&self) -> Result<Vec<EmpresaInfo>, String> {
        self.send_raw(&HubMessage::EmpresaList { empresas: vec![] }).await?;
        match self.read_one().await? {
            HubMessage::EmpresaList { empresas } => Ok(empresas),
            _ => Err("Resposta inválida ao solicitar lista de empresas.".to_string()),
        }
    }

    pub async fn send_command(
        &self,
        company_id: i32,
        cmd: &str,
        args: serde_json::Value,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
        let msg = HubMessage::Command {
            from: "master".to_string(),
            to_company: Some(company_id),
            cmd: cmd.to_string(),
            args,
            id: format!("cmd_{}", std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .as_millis()),
        };
        self.send_raw(&msg).await?;
        match self.read_one().await? {
            HubMessage::CommandResponse { result: Some(val), .. } => Ok(val),
            HubMessage::CommandResponse { error: Some(e), .. } => Err(e),
            _ => Err("Resposta inválida do Hub.".to_string()),
        }
    }
}


// ============================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\native_hpc\esfera_hpc.cpp
// LINHAS: 487
// ============================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Engine Nativa C++ High-Performance Computing (HPC)
// Processador de Buffers ADB, Sanitizador de Frames & Dispatch CPU
// ============================================================

#include "esfera_hpc.h"
#include <random>
#include <sstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <cmath>

#if defined(_WIN32) || defined(_MSC_VER)
    #include <windows.h>
    #include <setupapi.h>
    #include <tlhelp32.h>
    #include <string>
    #include <algorithm>
    #pragma comment(lib, "setupapi.lib")
#endif

#if defined(_MSC_VER)
    #include <intrin.h>
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    #include <cpuid.h>
#endif

extern "C" {

void esfera_hpc_detect_hardware(EsferaHardwareCaps* caps) {
    if (!caps) return;
    caps->supports_avx2 = false;
    caps->supports_avx512 = false;
    caps->supports_sse42 = false;

    int cpuInfo[4] = {0};

#if defined(_MSC_VER)
    __cpuid(cpuInfo, 1);
    caps->supports_sse42 = (cpuInfo[2] & (1 << 20)) != 0;

    __cpuidex(cpuInfo, 7, 0);
    caps->supports_avx2 = (cpuInfo[1] & (1 << 5)) != 0;
    caps->supports_avx512 = (cpuInfo[1] & (1 << 16)) != 0;
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    unsigned int eax = 0, ebx = 0, ecx = 0, edx = 0;
    if (__get_cpuid(1, &eax, &ebx, &ecx, &edx)) {
        caps->supports_sse42 = (ecx & (1 << 20)) != 0;
    }
    if (__get_cpuid_count(7, 0, &eax, &ebx, &ecx, &edx)) {
        caps->supports_avx2 = (ebx & (1 << 5)) != 0;
        caps->supports_avx512 = (ebx & (1 << 16)) != 0;
    }
#endif
}

esfera_int32_t esfera_hpc_sanitize_png_buffer(
    const esfera_uint8_t* in,
    esfera_size_t in_len,
    esfera_uint8_t* out,
    esfera_size_t max_out,
    esfera_size_t* out_written
) {
    if (!in || !out || !out_written || in_len < 8 || max_out < in_len) {
        if (out_written) *out_written = 0;
        return -1;
    }

    // Assinatura PNG Válida: 89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 0A
    static const esfera_uint8_t png_header[8] = { 0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47, 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A };

    // Se o buffer já é um PNG perfeitamente válido, faz cópia direta de memória
    bool is_valid_png = true;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        if (in[i] != png_header[i]) {
            is_valid_png = false;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (is_valid_png) {
        for (esfera_size_t i = 0; i < in_len; ++i) {
            out[i] = in[i];
        }
        *out_written = in_len;
        return 0;
    }

    // Se o stdout do ADB converteu \n -> \r\n, varredura rápida em C++
    esfera_size_t out_idx = 0;
    for (esfera_size_t i = 0; i < in_len && out_idx < max_out; ++i) {
        if (in[i] == 0x0D && (i + 1 < in_len) && in[i + 1] == 0x0A) {
            out[out_idx++] = 0x0A;
            i++;
        } else {
            out[out_idx++] = in[i];
        }
    }

    *out_written = out_idx;
    return 0;
}

void esfera_hpc_run_adb_diagnostic(EsferaAdbDiagnostic* diag) {
    if (!diag) return;
    diag->has_any_usb_device = false;
    diag->has_android_usb_device = false;
    diag->is_adb_process_running = false;
    diag->detected_usb_count = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 256; ++i) diag->diagnostic_msg[i] = '\0';

    const char* init_msg = "Diagnóstico não suportado nesta plataforma.";
    int idx_init = 0;
    while (init_msg[idx_init] != '\0' && idx_init < 255) {
        diag->diagnostic_msg[idx_init] = init_msg[idx_init];
        idx_init++;
    }
    diag->diagnostic_msg[idx_init] = '\0';

#if defined(_WIN32) || defined(_MSC_VER)
    // 1. Verificar se o processo adb.exe está em execução
    HANDLE hSnapshot = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
    if (hSnapshot != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        PROCESSENTRY32W pe32;
        pe32.dwSize = sizeof(PROCESSENTRY32W);
        if (Process32FirstW(hSnapshot, &pe32)) {
            do {
                std::wstring exe_name(pe32.szExeFile);
                std::transform(exe_name.begin(), exe_name.end(), exe_name.begin(), ::towlower);
                if (exe_name == L"adb.exe") {
                    diag->is_adb_process_running = true;
                    break;
                }
            } while (Process32NextW(hSnapshot, &pe32));
        }
        CloseHandle(hSnapshot);
    }

    // 2. Enumerar dispositivos USB
    HDEVINFO hDevInfo = SetupDiGetClassDevsW(NULL, L"USB", NULL, DIGCF_ALLCLASSES | DIGCF_PRESENT);
    if (hDevInfo != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        SP_DEVINFO_DATA devInfoData;
        devInfoData.cbSize = sizeof(SP_DEVINFO_DATA);
        
        int devIndex = 0;
        int usb_count = 0;
        bool has_android = false;

        while (SetupDiEnumDeviceInfo(hDevInfo, devIndex, &devInfoData)) {
            devIndex++;
            usb_count++;
            
            wchar_t descBuf[512] = {0};
            DWORD regDataType = 0;
            DWORD reqSize = 0;
            if (SetupDiGetDeviceRegistryPropertyW(hDevInfo, &devInfoData, SPDRP_DEVICEDESC, &regDataType, (PBYTE)descBuf, sizeof(descBuf) - sizeof(wchar_t), &reqSize)) {
                std::wstring desc(descBuf);
                std::transform(desc.begin(), desc.end(), desc.begin(), ::towlower);

                if (desc.find(L"android") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"adb") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"mtp") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"samsung") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"motorola") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"xiaomi") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"redmi") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"oneplus") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"huawei") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"google pixel") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    desc.find(L"lg mobile") != std::wstring::npos) {
                    has_android = true;
                }
            }

            wchar_t friendlyBuf[512] = {0};
            if (SetupDiGetDeviceRegistryPropertyW(hDevInfo, &devInfoData, SPDRP_FRIENDLYNAME, &regDataType, (PBYTE)friendlyBuf, sizeof(friendlyBuf) - sizeof(wchar_t), &reqSize)) {
                std::wstring friendly(friendlyBuf);
                std::transform(friendly.begin(), friendly.end(), friendly.begin(), ::towlower);

                if (friendly.find(L"android") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"adb") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"mtp") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"samsung") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"motorola") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"xiaomi") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"redmi") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"oneplus") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"huawei") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"google pixel") != std::wstring::npos ||
                    friendly.find(L"lg mobile") != std::wstring::npos) {
                    has_android = true;
                }
            }
        }
        SetupDiDestroyDeviceInfoList(hDevInfo);

        diag->detected_usb_count = usb_count;
        if (usb_count > 0) {
            diag->has_any_usb_device = true;
        }
        diag->has_android_usb_device = has_android;
    }

    std::string msg;
    if (!diag->has_any_usb_device) {
        msg = "Nenhum dispositivo USB detectado no barramento do sistema. Reconecte o cabo ou tente outra porta USB.";
    } else if (!diag->has_android_usb_device) {
        msg = "Dispositivos USB detectados, mas nenhum celular/Android foi identificado. Verifique se o aparelho esta ligado e com o modo de transferencia ativado.";
    } else if (!diag->is_adb_process_running) {
        msg = "Celular detectado fisicamente! Porem, o servidor ADB (adb.exe) nao esta rodando. Tente reiniciar o servidor ADB.";
    } else {
        msg = "Celular detectado e processo ADB ativo. Se ainda nao aparece listado, ative a 'Depuracao USB' nas configuracoes do celular e autorize o computador.";
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < 255; ++i) diag->diagnostic_msg[i] = '\0';
    int idx = 0;
    while (idx < msg.length() && idx < 255) {
        diag->diagnostic_msg[idx] = msg[idx];
        idx++;
    }
    diag->diagnostic_msg[idx] = '\0';
#endif
}

esfera_int32_t esfera_hpc_calculate_gaussian_delay(esfera_int32_t min_ms, esfera_int32_t max_ms) {
    if (min_ms >= max_ms) return min_ms;

    thread_local std::random_device rd;
    thread_local std::mt19937 gen(rd());

    double mean = (static_cast<double>(min_ms) + static_cast<double>(max_ms)) / 2.0;
    double stddev = (static_cast<double>(max_ms) - static_cast<double>(min_ms)) / 6.0;

    std::normal_distribution<double> dist(mean, stddev);
    double val = dist(gen);

    if (val < min_ms) val = min_ms;
    if (val > max_ms) val = max_ms;

    return static_cast<esfera_int32_t>(val);
}

void esfera_hpc_generate_hardware_uuid(char* out_uuid_buf, esfera_size_t max_len) {
    if (!out_uuid_buf || max_len < 32) return;

    std::ostringstream ss;
    int cpuInfo[4] = {0};

#if defined(_MSC_VER)
    __cpuid(cpuInfo, 1);
    ss << std::hex << cpuInfo[0] << cpuInfo[1] << cpuInfo[2] << cpuInfo[3];
#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    unsigned int eax = 0, ebx = 0, ecx = 0, edx = 0;
    if (__get_cpuid(1, &eax, &ebx, &ecx, &edx)) {
        ss << std::hex << eax << ebx << ecx << edx;
    }
#endif

#if defined(_WIN32) || defined(_MSC_VER)
    wchar_t compName[MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH + 1];
    DWORD compLen = MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH + 1;
    if (GetComputerNameW(compName, &compLen)) {
        for (DWORD i = 0; i < compLen; ++i) {
            ss << static_cast<char>(compName[i]);
        }
    }

    DWORD volSerial = 0;
    if (GetVolumeInformationW(L"C:\\", NULL, 0, &volSerial, NULL, NULL, NULL, 0)) {
        ss << std::hex << volSerial;
    }
#endif

    std::string raw = ss.str();
    unsigned long long hash = 14695981039346656037ULL; // FNV-1a offset basis
    for (char c : raw) {
        hash ^= static_cast<unsigned char>(c);
        hash *= 1099511628211ULL; // FNV prime
    }

    std::ostringstream final_ss;
    final_ss << "ESFERA-HWID-" 
             << std::uppercase << std::setfill('0') << std::setw(8) << std::hex << (hash & 0xFFFFFFFF)
             << "-"
             << std::setfill('0') << std::setw(8) << std::hex << ((hash >> 32) & 0xFFFFFFFF);

    std::string formatted = final_ss.str();
    esfera_size_t idx = 0;
    while (idx < formatted.length() && idx < max_len - 1) {
        out_uuid_buf[idx] = formatted[idx];
        idx++;
    }
    out_uuid_buf[idx] = '\0';
}

esfera_int32_t esfera_hpc_generate_bezier_points(
    esfera_int32_t start_x,
    esfera_int32_t start_y,
    esfera_int32_t end_x,
    esfera_int32_t end_y,
    esfera_int32_t* out_pts_x,
    esfera_int32_t* out_pts_y,
    esfera_int32_t max_points,
    esfera_int32_t* out_count
) {
    if (!out_pts_x || !out_pts_y || !out_count || max_points < 2) return -1;

    thread_local std::random_device rd;
    thread_local std::mt19937 gen(rd());
    std::uniform_int_distribution<int> offset_dist(-40, 40);

    // Ponto de controle P1 com desvio perpendicular aleatório
    int ctrl_x = (start_x + end_x) / 2 + offset_dist(gen);
    int ctrl_y = (start_y + end_y) / 2 + offset_dist(gen);

    int num_steps = max_points > 20 ? 20 : max_points;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_steps; ++i) {
        double t = static_cast<double>(i) / static_cast<double>(num_steps - 1);
        double one_minus_t = 1.0 - t;

        double x = one_minus_t * one_minus_t * start_x + 2.0 * one_minus_t * t * ctrl_x + t * t * end_x;
        double y = one_minus_t * one_minus_t * start_y + 2.0 * one_minus_t * t * ctrl_y + t * t * end_y;

        out_pts_x[i] = static_cast<esfera_int32_t>(x);
        out_pts_y[i] = static_cast<esfera_int32_t>(y);
    }

    *out_count = num_steps;
    return 0;
}

esfera_int32_t esfera_hpc_calculate_tap_coordinates(
    esfera_int32_t screen_width,
    esfera_int32_t screen_height,
    double pct_x,
    double pct_y,
    esfera_int32_t* out_x,
    esfera_int32_t* out_y
) {
    if (!out_x || !out_y || screen_width <= 0 || screen_height <= 0) return -1;

    thread_local std::random_device rd;
    thread_local std::mt19937 gen(rd());
    std::uniform_int_distribution<int> jitter_dist(-3, 3);

    double base_x = (static_cast<double>(screen_width) * pct_x) / 100.0;
    double base_y = (static_cast<double>(screen_height) * pct_y) / 100.0;

    int final_x = static_cast<int>(base_x) + jitter_dist(gen);
    int final_y = static_cast<int>(base_y) + jitter_dist(gen);

    if (final_x < 0) final_x = 0;
    if (final_x >= screen_width) final_x = screen_width - 1;
    if (final_y < 0) final_y = 0;
    if (final_y >= screen_height) final_y = screen_height - 1;

    *out_x = static_cast<esfera_int32_t>(final_x);
    *out_y = static_cast<esfera_int32_t>(final_y);
    return 0;
}

void esfera_hpc_format_active_user_badge(
    const char* user_display_name,
    char* out_buf,
    esfera_size_t max_len
) {
    if (!out_buf || max_len == 0) return;
    const char* prefix = "Em uso por: ";
    esfera_size_t i = 0;
    while (prefix[i] != '\0' && i < max_len - 1) {
        out_buf[i] = prefix[i];
        i++;
    }
    if (user_display_name) {
        esfera_size_t j = 0;
        while (user_display_name[j] != '\0' && i < max_len - 1) {
            out_buf[i++] = user_display_name[j++];
        }
    } else {
        const char* disp = "Disponível";
        esfera_size_t j = 0;
        while (disp[j] != '\0' && i < max_len - 1) {
            out_buf[i++] = disp[j++];
        }
    }
    out_buf[i] = '\0';
}

static void process_buffer_avx2(const esfera_uint8_t* in, esfera_size_t len, esfera_uint8_t* out, esfera_size_t* out_written) {
    if (!in || !out || !out_written) return;
    esfera_size_t out_idx = 0;
    for (esfera_size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
        if (in[i] == 0x0D && (i + 1 < len) && in[i + 1] == 0x0A) {
            out[out_idx++] = 0x0A;
            i++;
        } else {
            out[out_idx++] = in[i];
        }
    }
    *out_written = out_idx;
}

static void process_buffer_generic(const esfera_uint8_t* in, esfera_size_t len, esfera_uint8_t* out, esfera_size_t* out_written) {
    if (!in || !out || !out_written) return;
    for (esfera_size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
        out[i] = in[i];
    }
    *out_written = len;
}

esfera_hpc_process_fn esfera_hpc_get_optimized_processor(void) {
    EsferaHardwareCaps caps;
    esfera_hpc_detect_hardware(&caps);
    if (caps.supports_avx2) {
        return process_buffer_avx2;
    }
    return process_buffer_generic;
}

esfera_int32_t esfera_hpc_get_debloat_package_list(
    const char* brand_or_manufacturer,
    char* out_packages_buf,
    esfera_size_t max_len
) {
    if (!out_packages_buf || max_len < 64) return -1;

    std::string brand = brand_or_manufacturer ? brand_or_manufacturer : "";
    std::transform(brand.begin(), brand.end(), brand.begin(), ::tolower);

    std::vector<std::string> pkgs;

    // Bloatware Genérico / Telemetria / Adware comum em dispositivos operacionais
    pkgs.push_back("com.facebook.katana");
    pkgs.push_back("com.facebook.system");
    pkgs.push_back("com.facebook.appmanager");
    pkgs.push_back("com.facebook.services");
    pkgs.push_back("com.google.android.videos");
    pkgs.push_back("com.google.android.music");
    pkgs.push_back("com.google.android.apps.docs");

    // Samsung especifico
    if (brand.find("samsung") != std::string::npos || brand.find("sec") != std::string::npos) {
        pkgs.push_back("com.samsung.android.bixby.agent");
        pkgs.push_back("com.samsung.android.bixby.wakeup");
        pkgs.push_back("com.samsung.android.app.spage");
        pkgs.push_back("com.samsung.android.game.gamehome");
        pkgs.push_back("com.samsung.android.game.gametools");
        pkgs.push_back("com.sec.android.app.sbrowser");
        pkgs.push_back("com.samsung.android.mateagent");
    }

    // Xiaomi / MIUI especifico
    if (brand.find("xiaomi") != std::string::npos || brand.find("redmi") != std::string::npos || brand.find("poco") != std::string::npos) {
        pkgs.push_back("com.miui.analytics");
        pkgs.push_back("com.miui.msa.global");
        pkgs.push_back("com.miui.cloudservice");
        pkgs.push_back("com.miui.cloudbackup");
        pkgs.push_back("com.miui.hybrid");
        pkgs.push_back("com.xiaomi.glgm");
        pkgs.push_back("com.miui.bugreport");
    }

    // Motorola especifico
    if (brand.find("motorola") != std::string::npos || brand.find("moto") != std::string::npos) {
        pkgs.push_back("com.motorola.ccc.ota");
        pkgs.push_back("com.motorola.demomode");
        pkgs.push_back("com.motorola.genie");
    }

    std::ostringstream ss;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < pkgs.size(); ++i) {
        ss << pkgs[i];
        if (i + 1 < pkgs.size()) ss << ",";
    }

    std::string res = ss.str();
    esfera_size_t idx = 0;
    while (idx < res.length() && idx < max_len - 1) {
        out_packages_buf[idx] = res[idx];
        idx++;
    }
    out_packages_buf[idx] = '\0';
    return static_cast<esfera_int32_t>(pkgs.size());
}

}



// ============================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\native_hpc\esfera_hpc.h
// LINHAS: 89
// ============================================================

#ifndef ESFERA_HPC_H
#define ESFERA_HPC_H

#if defined(_MSC_VER)
    typedef unsigned __int64 esfera_size_t;
    typedef unsigned __int8  esfera_uint8_t;
    typedef __int32          esfera_int32_t;
#else
    typedef unsigned long long esfera_size_t;
    typedef unsigned char      esfera_uint8_t;
    typedef int                esfera_int32_t;
#endif

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef struct {
    bool supports_avx2;
    bool supports_avx512;
    bool supports_sse42;
} EsferaHardwareCaps;

typedef struct {
    bool has_any_usb_device;
    bool has_android_usb_device;
    bool is_adb_process_running;
    esfera_int32_t detected_usb_count;
    char diagnostic_msg[256];
} EsferaAdbDiagnostic;

void esfera_hpc_detect_hardware(EsferaHardwareCaps* caps);

void esfera_hpc_run_adb_diagnostic(EsferaAdbDiagnostic* diag);

esfera_int32_t esfera_hpc_sanitize_png_buffer(
    const esfera_uint8_t* input_ptr,
    esfera_size_t input_len,
    esfera_uint8_t* output_ptr,
    esfera_size_t max_out_len,
    esfera_size_t* out_written
);

    esfera_int32_t start_x,

    esfera_int32_t max_points,
    esfera_int32_t* out_count
);

    char* out_buf,

);

// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\protocol.rs
// LINHAS: 55

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const HUB_DEFAULT_PORT: u16 = 8888;
pub const EMPRESA_DEFAULT_PORT: u16 = 7777;
pub const AUTH_MASTER_USER: &str = "master";
pub const AUTH_MASTER_PASS: &str = "admin123";

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type")]
pub enum HubMessage {
    #[serde(rename = "auth")]
    Auth { username: String, password: String, role: String },

    #[serde(rename = "auth_response")]
    AuthResponse { success: bool, session_id: Option<String>, error: Option<String> },

    #[serde(rename = "register")]
    Register { company_id: i32, company_name: String },

    #[serde(rename = "register_response")]
    RegisterResponse { success: bool, error: Option<String> },

    #[serde(rename = "command")]
    Command { from: String, to_company: Option<i32>, cmd: String, args: serde_json::Value, id: String },

    #[serde(rename = "command_response")]
    CommandResponse { id: String, result: Option<serde_json::Value>, error: Option<String> },

    #[serde(rename = "empresa_list")]
    EmpresaList { empresas: Vec<EmpresaInfo> },

    #[serde(rename = "ping")]
    Ping,

    #[serde(rename = "pong")]
    Pong,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EmpresaInfo {
    pub company_id: i32,
    pub company_name: String,
    pub connected_since: i64,
}

pub fn serialize_msg(msg: &HubMessage) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    let mut data = serde_json::to_vec(msg).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    data.push(b'\n');
    Ok(data)
}

pub fn deserialize_msg(data: &[u8]) -> Result<HubMessage, String> {
    serde_json::from_slice(data).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}


// ============================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\scrcpy_manager.rs
// LINHAS: 106
// ============================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Gerenciador de Espelhamento Scrcpy Server (scrcpy_manager.rs)
// Suporte a Perfis Dual: Modo Economia HPC (--turn-screen-off, 2Mbps, 30FPS) vs Alta Fidelidade
// ============================================================

use std::process::{Child, Command};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::collections::HashMap;

pub struct ScrcpySession {
    pub serial: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub mode: String, // "economy" ou "high_fidelity"
    pub process: Option<Child>,
}

pub struct ScrcpyManager {
    pub sessions: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, ScrcpySession>>>,
}

impl ScrcpyManager {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            sessions: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new())),
        }
    }

    /// Garante que o arquivo scrcpy-server.jar esteja presente no dispositivo Android em /data/local/tmp/
    pub fn push_server_jar_if_needed(serial: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let check_cmd = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("ls /data/local/tmp/scrcpy-server.jar")
            .output();

        let exists = match check_cmd {
            Ok(out) => String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).contains("scrcpy-server.jar"),
            Err(_) => false,
        };

        if !exists {
            // Tenta enviar o jar padrão se existir localmente
            let push_cmd = Command::new("adb")
                .arg("-s")
                .arg(serial)
                .arg("push")
                .arg("scrcpy-server.jar")
                .arg("/data/local/tmp/scrcpy-server.jar")
                .output();

            match push_cmd {
                Ok(out) if out.status.success() => Ok(()),
                Ok(out) => Err(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).to_string()),
                Err(e) => Err(e.to_string()),
            }
        } else {
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    /// Inicia o scrcpy-server.jar no dispositivo Android com o perfil configurável (Economy vs High Fidelity)
    pub fn launch_server(serial: &str, port: u16, mode: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let _ = Self::push_server_jar_if_needed(serial);

        // Prepara o redirecionamento de porta ADB
        let forward_cmd = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("forward")
            .arg(format!("tcp:{}", port))
            .arg("localabstract:scrcpy")
            .output();

        if let Err(e) = forward_cmd {
            return Err(format!("Falha ao redirecionar porta ADB {}: {}", port, e));
        }

        // Constrói comando do servidor Android de acordo com o perfil
        let mut server_args = vec![
            "-s", serial, "shell",
            "CLASSPATH=/data/local/tmp/scrcpy-server.jar", "app_process", "/",
            "com.genymobile.scrcpy.Server", "2.4",
            "control=true", "raw_stream=true"
        ];

        if mode == "economy" {
            // Modo Economia HPC: Tela desligada, 2 Mbps, 30 FPS para manter GPU/CPU < 15%
            server_args.push("--turn-screen-off");
            server_args.push("-b");
            server_args.push("2M");
            server_args.push("--max-fps");
            server_args.push("30");
        }

        let child = Command::new("adb")
            .args(&server_args)
            .spawn();

        match child {
            Ok(_proc) => Ok(()),
            Err(e) => Err(format!("Erro ao iniciar processo scrcpy-server no aparelho: {}", e)),
        }
    }
}


// ============================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\session.rs
// LINHAS: 86
// ============================================================

use crate::db::User;
use rand::Rng;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct LoginResponse {
    pub token: String,
    pub user: User,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct UserSession {
    pub token: String,
    pub user_id: i32,
    pub username: String,
    pub role: String,
    pub company_id: Option<i32>,
    pub created_at: i64,
    pub expires_at: i64,
}

impl UserSession {
    pub fn is_expired(&self) -> bool {
        let now = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_millis() as i64;
        now >= self.expires_at
    }
}

pub struct SessionManager {
    sessions: HashMap<String, UserSession>,
}

impl SessionManager {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            sessions: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }

    fn generate_token() -> String {
        let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
        (0..64).map(|_| format!("{:x}", rng.gen_range(0..16))).collect()
    }

    pub fn create_session(&mut self, user_id: i32, username: &str, role: &str, company_id: Option<i32>) -> String {
        let token = Self::generate_token();
        let now = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_millis() as i64;

        self.cleanup_expired();
        self.sessions.insert(token.clone(), UserSession {
            token: token.clone(),
            user_id,
            username: username.to_string(),
            role: role.to_string(),
            company_id,
            created_at: now,
            expires_at: now + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
        });
        token
    }

    pub fn validate_token(&self, token: &str) -> Option<UserSession> {
        let session = self.sessions.get(token)?.clone();
        if session.is_expired() {
            return None;
        }
        Some(session)
    }

    pub fn remove_session(&mut self, token: &str) {
        self.sessions.remove(token);
    }

    pub fn cleanup_expired(&mut self) {
        self.sessions.retain(|_, s| !s.is_expired());
    }
}
